     1	import { useState } from 'react';
     2	import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
     3	import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
     4	import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
     5	import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
     6	import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
     7	import { toast } from 'sonner';
     8	import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
     9	import { createClassroomItem } from '@/lib/classroomItems';
    10	
    11	interface Props {
    12	  studentId: string;
    13	  adminUserId: string;
    14	  onClose: () => void;
    15	}
    16	
    17	export default function RecapComposer({ studentId, adminUserId, onClose }: Props) {
    18	  const qc = useQueryClient();
    19	  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
    20	  const [body, setBody] = useState('');
    21	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
    22	
    23	  const submit = async () => {
    24	    if (!title.trim() || !body.trim()) {
    25	      toast.error('Title and body required');
    26	      return;
    27	    }
    28	    setSubmitting(true);
    29	    try {
    30	      await createClassroomItem({
    31	        studentId,
    32	        createdBy: adminUserId,
    33	        type: 'session_recap',
    34	        title: title.trim(),
    35	        contentMarkdown: body,
    36	        visibleToStudent: true,
    37	        status: 'assigned',
    38	      });
    39	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['classroom-items-admin', studentId] });
    40	      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['classroom-items'] });
    41	      toast.success('Recap posted to Classroom');
    42	      onClose();
    43	    } catch (e: any) {
    44	      toast.error(e?.message ?? 'Failed to post recap');
    45	    } finally {
    46	      setSubmitting(false);
    47	    }
    48	  };
    49	
    50	  return (
    51	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-end md:items-center justify-center p-4">
    52	      <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 shadow-xl p-5 space-y-4">
    53	        <div>
    54	          <h3 className="text-base font-semibold">Post a session recap</h3>
    55	          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
    56	            Posts immediately to the student's Classroom under Session recaps.
    57	          </p>
    58	        </div>
    59	        <div className="space-y-3">
    60	          <div>
    61	            <Label className="text-xs">Title</Label>
    62	            <Input
    63	              value={title}
    64	              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
    65	              className="mt-1"
    66	              placeholder="Today's session"
    67	            />
    68	          </div>
    69	          <div>
    70	            <Label className="text-xs">Body</Label>
    71	            <Textarea
    72	              value={body}
    73	              onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)}
    74	              rows={10}
    75	              className="mt-1 text-sm"
    76	              placeholder="Key takeaways, what we worked on, next steps."
    77	            />
    78	          </div>
    79	        </div>
    80	        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-1">
    81	          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onClose} disabled={submitting}>
    82	            Cancel
    83	          </Button>
    84	          <Button size="sm" onClick={submit} disabled={submitting}>
    85	            {submitting && <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />}
    86	            Post recap
    87	          </Button>
    88	        </div>
    89	      </div>
    90	    </div>
    91	  );
    92	}

    50	export default function StudentNotes({ studentId, studentName }: Props) {

    61	      } else {
    62	        const arr = buckets.get(it.context_type) ?? [];
    63	        arr.push(it);

     3	import { ArrowLeft, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
     4	import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
     5	import { useClassroomItems } from '@/hooks/useClassroomItems';

     9	
    10	/**
    11	 * /classroom/item/:itemId
    12	 *
    13	 * For action-y items (drill / homework / bootcamp) this page is a
    14	 * resolver: it redirects into the underlying flow. For content items
    15	 * (note / announcement / recap) it renders the body directly.
    16	 */
    17	export default function ClassroomItem() {
    18	  const { itemId } = useParams<{ itemId: string }>();
    19	  const navigate = useNavigate();
    20	  const { data: items = [], isLoading } = useClassroomItems();
    21	
    22	  const item = useMemo(
    23	    () => items.find((i) => i.id === itemId) ?? null,
    24	    [items, itemId],
    25	  );
    26	
    27	  // Action items redirect.
    28	  useEffect(() => {
    29	    if (!item) return;
    30	    // A self-contained item IS its own page, so redirecting it away from
    31	    // itself is a bounce back to Home and the student never sees the content.
    32	    if (isSelfContainedItem(item)) return;
    33	    if (
    34	      item.type === 'drill' ||
    35	      item.type === 'homework' ||
    36	      item.type === 'bootcamp_module' ||
    37	      item.type === 'weekly_plan_task'
    38	    ) {
    39	      const route = resolveItemRoute(item);
    40	      if (route) navigate(route, { replace: true });
    41	    }
    42	  }, [item, navigate]);
    43	
    44	  if (isLoading) {
    45	    return (
    46	      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground">
    47	        <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> Loading…
    48	      </div>
    49	    );
    50	  }

    52	    return (

    60	      </div>

    47	/**
    48	 * Student-side: returns visible, unlocked classroom_items for the signed-in
    49	 * user. Keep the explicit student_id filter even though RLS also protects
    50	 * student accounts: admins can read every row, and may still open student-side
    51	 * routes while testing. Without this scope, an admin sees every student's work
    52	 * combined into one calendar.
    53	 */
    54	export function useClassroomItems() {
    55	  const { user } = useAuth();
    56	  return useQuery<ClassroomItem[]>({
    57	    queryKey: ['classroom-items', user?.id],
    58	    enabled: !!user,
    59	    staleTime: 60_000,
    60	    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,
    61	    queryFn: async () => {
    62	      const { data, error } = await supabase
    63	        .from('classroom_items')
    64	        .select('*')
    65	        .eq('student_id', user!.id)
    66	        .order('priority', { ascending: false })
    67	        .order('due_at', { ascending: true, nullsFirst: false })
    68	        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
    69	      if (error) throw error;
    70	      return (data ?? []) as ClassroomItem[];
    71	    },
    72	  });
    73	}

    83	    queryFn: async () => {

    91	    },

     1	import ReactMarkdown from 'react-markdown';
     2	import type { Components } from 'react-markdown';
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * The single markdown renderer for every piece of RC content: passage text,
     6	 * summaries, prompts, choice text, and explanation bodies.
     7	 *
     8	 * Deliberately narrow: paragraphs, bold, italics, line breaks, blockquotes and
     9	 * lists render; links, images, headings and raw HTML do not. Lists are allowed
    10	 * because authored explanation blocks genuinely contain them, and dropping them
    11	 * would mangle the author's content.
    12	 */
    13	
    14	const components: Components = {
    15	  a: ({ children }) => <>{children}</>,
    16	  img: () => null,
    17	  // Authored content occasionally uses a heading where a bold line is meant;
    18	  // render it as an ordinary paragraph rather than injecting document structure.
    19	  h1: ({ children }) => <p>{children}</p>,
    20	  h2: ({ children }) => <p>{children}</p>,
    21	  h3: ({ children }) => <p>{children}</p>,
    22	  h4: ({ children }) => <p>{children}</p>,
    23	  h5: ({ children }) => <p>{children}</p>,
    24	  h6: ({ children }) => <p>{children}</p>,
    25	};
    26	
    27	interface RcMarkdownProps {
    28	  children: string | null | undefined;
    29	  className?: string;
    30	}
    31	
    32	export function RcMarkdown({ children, className }: RcMarkdownProps) {
    33	  if (!children) return null;
    34	  return (
    35	    <div className={className}>
    36	      <ReactMarkdown components={components} skipHtml>
    37	        {children}
    38	      </ReactMarkdown>
    39	    </div>
    40	  );
    41	}
    42	
    43	export default RcMarkdown;

=== CONTEXT.md ===
# Classroom

The student's own room: the first surface a 1:1 student lands on. It is a reading surface, not a workspace. Its language is second person, and the coach is never named in the furniture.

## Terms

**Classroom frame** — the persistent layout every Classroom route is drawn in: the cream left rail, an optional middle list, and the white reading surface. Implemented by `ClassroomLayout`. Navigating between surfaces it draws swaps the content only and never remounts the rail. A page "sits in the Classroom frame" regardless of its URL; `/flagged` and `/journal` both do.

**StudentShell** — the older, separate frame with its own 240px rail, still drawing `/mainhub`, `/practice` and the rest. A student moving between a Classroom-frame page and a StudentShell page sees the whole page rebuild. Reducing that boundary is ongoing work, one surface at a time.

**Reading column** — the white content column. Most routes use one reading column on the right. A Wrong answers detail uses a four-column Classroom frame: cream rail, entries list, vertical question, and review panel. Below its journal-only breakpoints the entries list becomes an overlay and the review panel moves under the question.

**Drill landing workspace** — the pre-question surface at `/classroom/drill`, drawn inside the Classroom frame. It includes drill selection, launch controls, practice insights, and today's work; it excludes the official question-answering experience.
_Avoid_: Drill page (when the question experience could be confused with the landing surface), Mainhub

**Question finder** — the one control that turns something a student can say about a question into that question opened. It takes free text, matched against question text, and it takes a question address (PT 90, Section 1, Question 12) typed or picked from PrepTest / Section / Question selectors. It searches both banks and groups its results by bank, Logical Reasoning first, because a hit lands differently in each: an LR hit opens that one question, an RC hit opens its whole passage with every question on it. The finder is a finding aid, not the scope of the work it opens. It appears on Classroom Home and in the Drill landing workspace; it never builds a set, it hands that to the Drill console.
_Avoid_: search bar, command palette, spotlight, quick access, find bar

**Drill console** — the control surface inside the Drill landing workspace where a student assembles a set: the filters, the preview of the exact set, and the start action. Finding one named question is not its job; that is the Question finder.
_Avoid_: Control pane, picker card

**Drill canvas** — the route-specific visual composition to the right of the persistent Classroom rail, containing the Drill hero, Drill console, practice/insight workspace, and Today focus. The Classroom rail is not part of the canvas.
_Avoid_: Drill side pane, dashboard grid

**Section** — a middle-column list paired with the reading column: Notes and recaps, Assigned work, Flagged. A section is picked in the rail, lists its items in the middle column, and shows the chosen item in the reading column.

**Flag** — a come-back-to-this marker the student raises *during* a question, from a drill or a blind review. It records intent to return, and says nothing about whether the question was answered correctly. Stored in `flagged_questions`.

**Flagged question** — a question carrying a flag. The Flagged section is the student's unfinished business: questions they chose to set aside. Clearing a flag is the student's own act (Unflag), not something the system does when they get the question right.

**Wrong answer** — a question the student actually missed, recorded in `wrong_answer_journal` and worked through in Wrong answers at `/journal`. Distinct from a flag in both meaning and storage: a flag is chosen, a wrong answer is earned. A question can be both, one, or neither. Do not treat the two lists as views of the same thing.

**Journal entry** — the review of one wrong answer at `/journal/:qid`. The URL is the selection: it resolves only by `qid`, never by the journal row id, and `/journal` does not silently select the first entry. Its review fields autosave without a visible save state.

**Ask** — sending a question to the coach from the surface the student is looking at, with their own comment attached. Always labelled "Ask", never with the coach's name; the name belongs in messaging itself, not in the furniture that leads to it.

## Boundaries

The Classroom owns presentation and the student's own annotations of their work. It does not own the work: assignments, drills, attempts and the question bank all belong to their own contexts and are read here, not authored here.

174 .impeccable/surfaces/classroom.md
# Surface brief: the classroom (`.classroom-root`)

**Scope.** Everything behind the `.classroom-root` wrapper: the rail, the section list,
the stage, and the routes under `/classroom`, `/flagged` and `/journal` that render
inside that shell. Not the bootcamps or the Activity Centre worlds, which have their own
wrappers.

**Register.** Product. Design serves the work; the student is here to read what their
coach wrote and to find the next thing to do.

**Status.** Sanctioned world, added to DESIGN.md's scoped-world list on 2026-09-08 by
Joshua's decision. The audit found the classroom running on its own typeface and a warm
palette while the app-wide record described the Workroom as neutral and Lexend-only. The
decision was that the code was right and the record was stale. This file is the record
catching up.

## What makes it its own world

**A warm paper ground, not the Workroom's neutral.** `--classroom-page` is `#ffffff`
with `--classroom-line` at `#f3e3d6` and `--classroom-hover` a warm translucent cream.
The rail reads as a study rather than an admin panel, and both independent design
assessments in the 2026-09-08 audit named that warmth as a genuine asset rather than
drift.

**Outfit, not Lexend.** `classroom.css` sets `font-family: "Outfit"` on the wrapper. The
app-wide Workroom face is Lexend, and Lexend renders nowhere inside the classroom. That
is deliberate as of this brief. Outfit is also the question lock, so LSAT text and the
room it sits in share a face, and a passage does not change shape when the student moves
between reading a note and reading a stimulus.

**IBM Plex Mono for instrument labels.** The rail group labels, the chevrons, the brand
lockup and the unread badge. Swapped from Roboto Mono on 2026-09-08: Roboto Mono was
undeclared anywhere in the design record, and IBM Plex Mono is the mono this project
already documents and already loads.

## Tokens this world owns

| Token | Light | Dark | Use |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--classroom-page` | `#ffffff` | `#0f0e17` | the ground |
| `--classroom-surface` | `#ffffff` | `#0a0913` | panel and card ground |
| `--classroom-ink` | `#43403c` | `#fffffe` | primary text |
| `--classroom-ink-body` | `#43403c` | `#e5e5e5` | body copy |
| `--classroom-meta-text` | `#6d6a67` | `#a7a9be` | **any text that is muted** |
| `--classroom-meta-soft` | `rgba(67,64,60,.4)` | `#a7a9be` | **fills and borders only, never text** |
| `--classroom-line` | `#f3e3d6` | `#17161c` | hairline separation |
| `--classroom-line-strong` | `#9c8f85` | `#646176` | a border that is itself the affordance (WCAG 1.4.11) |
| `--classroom-line-on-tint` | `#8a7d72` | `#7a7789` | the same job, for a control whose ground is a tint rather than the page |
| `--classroom-accent` | `#a25704` | `#ff8906` | a fill that carries text: primary button, unread badge, unread dot |
| `--classroom-accent-ink` | `#ffffff` | `#0f0e17` | the label on that fill |
| `--classroom-link` | `#ff8906` | `#ff8906` | **decorative fills only** |
| `--classroom-link-text` | `#a25704` | `#ff8906` | orange that carries words |

## Named rules

**The text/fill split is the load-bearing rule here.** `--classroom-meta` and
`--classroom-meta-soft` are tuned as fills and measure 1.85:1 and 2.10:1 as text on the
page. Every muted *word* resolves from `--classroom-meta-text`. The same split governs
`--classroom-link` and `--classroom-link-text`. Getting this wrong put every date, every
rail group label and the unread badge below the AA floor for months.

**Opacity multiplies against the colour.** Swapping a token is not enough if an
`opacity` sits on the same rule. `.classroom-nav-group > b` was `#6d6a67` at `0.7` and
still measured 2.92:1. Muted text carries its colour in the token, not in an alpha.

**A themed pair, not a shared colour, for anything on an accent fill.** The amber that
works on the light ground is `#a25704`; on the dark ground it is `#ff8906` with near-black
on top. Reusing one value across both themes trades a light failure for a dark one.

**The section list is reached from the chrome, not from the reading column.** The
"Open the list" trigger that used to sit inside `.classroom-column` was removed on
2026-09-09. Its stated justification - that on a detail route "nothing else reaches the
list" - was not true: between 1024px and 1279px the sidebar is fully on screen and its
own section item runs the same `openSection`, which opens the same drawer; and every
note already ends with "Back to notes and recaps". It cost 48px plus a 32px margin above
the article’s own dateline and h1, which measured 23% of a 390px screen and 25% of an
1100px one before a word the coach wrote.

What carries the job now: the sidebar above 1023px, and below it
`.classroom-mobile-bar-trigger`, the sticky bar’s section name promoted to a button. The

    29	function formatClassroomDate(value: string | Date) {
    30	  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
    31	    day: "numeric",
    32	    month: "long",
    33	    year: "numeric",
    34	  }).format(typeof value === "string" ? new Date(value) : value);
    35	}

    37	/**
    38	 * Row and heading labels for note types. The raw enum leaked into the UI, so a
    39	 * student read "note" and "smartboard recap".
    40	 *
    41	 * `smartboard_recap` is a dead type as far as students are concerned: Joshua
    42	 * confirmed 2026-09-09 that boards are not attached to accounts, so no student
    43	 * ever sees this label. It stays because the teacher side can still write the
    44	 * type and an unmapped value would fall through to the raw enum. Do not spend
    45	 * effort wording it.
    46	 */
    47	const NOTE_KIND_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
    48	  note: "Note",
    49	  session_recap: "Session recap",
    50	  smartboard_recap: "Board from your session",
    51	  announcement: "Announcement",
    52	};
    53	
    54	function noteKind(type: string) {
    55	  return NOTE_KIND_LABELS[type] ?? type.replace(/_/g, " ");
    56	}
    57	
    58	/**
    59	 * First ~55 words of a note, for the landing preview.
    60	 *
    61	 * Notes are authored HTML and may carry their own <style>, so the tags are
    62	 * stripped through a detached element rather than a regex, and any <style> or
    63	 * <script> subtree is dropped first so CSS text never leaks into the excerpt.
    64	 */
    65	function noteExcerpt(item: {
    66	  description: string | null;
    67	  content_markdown?: string | null;
    68	  content_html: string | null;
    69	}) {
    70	  const source = item.description || item.content_markdown || item.content_html || "";
    71	  if (!source) return "";
    72	  let text = source;
    73	  if (/[<>]/.test(source)) {
    74	    const holder = document.createElement("div");
    75	    holder.innerHTML = source;
    76	    holder.querySelectorAll("style, script").forEach((node) => node.remove());
    77	    text = holder.textContent ?? "";
    78	  }
    79	  const words = text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().split(" ").filter(Boolean);
    80	  if (words.length === 0) return "";
    81	  return words.length <= 55 ? words.join(" ") : `${words.slice(0, 55).join(" ")}…`;
    82	}
    83	
    84	/** Below this, a flat list is easier to scan than a grouped one. */
    85	const MONTH_GROUP_THRESHOLD = 12;
    86	
    87	/**
    88	 * Read notes bucketed by the month they were written, newest month first.
    89	 * Input is already sorted newest-first by groupNotes, so insertion order is
    90	 * the display order and no second sort is needed.
    91	 */
    92	function groupNotesByMonth<T extends { created_at: string }>(notes: T[]) {
    93	  const buckets = new Map<string, { key: string; label: string; notes: T[] }>();
    94	  for (const note of notes) {
    95	    const date = new Date(note.created_at);
    96	    const key = `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth()}`;
    97	    let bucket = buckets.get(key);
    98	    if (!bucket) {
    99	      bucket = {
   100	        key,
   101	        label: new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
   102	          month: "long",
   103	          year: "numeric",
   104	        }).format(date),
   105	        notes: [],
   106	      };
   107	      buckets.set(key, bucket);
   108	    }
   109	    bucket.notes.push(note);
   110	  }
   111	  return [...buckets.values()];
   112	}

   242	      {/* `item.type.replace(/_/g," ")` printed the raw column value, so students
   243	          read "note" and "smartboard recap". NOTE_KIND_LABELS gives each type a
   244	          name a student would actually use. */}
   245	      <DetailHeading
   246	        date={item.created_at}

   296	/**
   297	 * The newest note, opened far enough to be worth landing on.
   298	 *
   299	 * The stage used to render a dateline, an <h1> and a one-line count into a
   300	 * 720px column and stop, so the widest region of the page carried the least
   301	 * information and read as a page that had failed to load. Principle 2 asks the
   302	 * product to compute the next step rather than present a menu; the next step
   303	 * here is almost always the note that just arrived.
   304	 *
   305	 * Deliberately not a card. The stage has no card around it, and a bordered box
   306	 * holding the only content on the page would be a container for its own sake.
   307	 */
   308	function FeaturedNote({
   309	  note,
   310	  unread,
   311	}: {
   312	  note: ReturnType<typeof groupNotes>["unread"][number];
   313	  unread: boolean;
   314	}) {

   316	  const excerpt = React.useMemo(() => noteExcerpt(note), [note]);
   317	  const open = () => navigate(`/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(note.id)}`);
   318	
   319	  return (
   320	    <section className="classroom-featured-note" aria-labelledby="featured-note-title">
   321	      <p className="classroom-featured-note-meta">
   322	        {unread ? "Newest, unread" : "Most recent"}
   323	        <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
   324	        {noteKind(note.type)}
   325	        <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
   326	        <time dateTime={note.created_at}>{formatClassroomDate(note.created_at)}</time>
   327	      </p>
   328	      <h2 className="classroom-featured-note-title" id="featured-note-title">
   329	        {note.title}
   330	      </h2>
   331	      {excerpt ? <p className="classroom-featured-note-excerpt">{excerpt}</p> : null}
   332	      <button
   333	        type="button"
   334	        className="classroom-button classroom-button-primary"
   335	        onClick={open}
   336	      >
   337	        Read it
   338	      </button>
   339	    </section>
   340	  );
   341	}

   343	export function ClassroomRecapsView() {
   344	  const query = useClassroomItems();
   345	  const { unread, read } = groupNotes(query.data ?? []);
   346	  const total = unread.length + read.length;
   347	  // Unread first, because the reason to open this page is almost always
   348	  // something that just arrived. Falls back to the newest read note so the
   349	  // stage still says something once the student is caught up.
   350	  const featured = unread[0] ?? read[0] ?? null;
   351	
   352	  // The page promises two categories and the feed carries four types, none of
   353	  // which the row showed. A session recap, an announcement and a board from a
   354	  // session were indistinguishable until opened.
   355	  const row = (note: (typeof unread)[number], isUnread: boolean) => ({
   356	    key: note.id,
   357	    title: note.title,
   358	    meta: `${noteKind(note.type)} · ${shortDate(note.created_at)}`,
   359	    route: `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(note.id)}`,
   360	    unread: isUnread,
   361	  });
   362	
   363	  return (
   364	    <>
   365	      {/* No dateline on an index. It printed `new Date()`, so it stamped the
   366	          page with the day the student happened to visit while sitting in the
   367	          slot that, on a note, holds the day Joshua wrote it. It was the one
   368	          number on the page that did not trace to stored data. */}
   369	      <h1 className="classroom-title">Notes and recaps</h1>
   370	      {query.isLoading && !query.data ? <DetailLoading /> : null}
   371	      {query.error ? (
   372	        <DetailError
   373	          message="Could not load your notes and recaps."
   374	          retry={() => void query.refetch()}
   375	        />
   376	      ) : null}
   377	      {/* The zero case is a state, not an absence. Without a branch here the
   378	          page rendered a heading and stopped, which is the surface a brand new
   379	          student is most likely to open first. The wording is the line already
   380	          shipped by RecapsSection so the two places cannot drift, and it claims
   381	          nothing that is not true: it says where recaps come from and stops. */}
   382	      {!query.isLoading && !query.error && total === 0 ? (

   387	      {!query.isLoading && !query.error && total > 0 ? (

   401	          {unread.length > 0 ? (

   408	          {/* Read notes group by month once the list is long enough that a flat

   411	              filter while this list got nothing. Grouping beats a search box for
   412	              a chronological archive: a student looks for "the one after the
   413	              February session", not for a word. Below the threshold a flat list
   414	              is clearer, so the headings stay out of the way. */}
   415	          {read.length > MONTH_GROUP_THRESHOLD
   416	            ? groupNotesByMonth(read).map((group) => (

   422	                />

   424	            : read.length > 0 ? (

   427	                heading={unread.length > 0 ? "Read" : undefined}
   428	                items={read.map((note) => row(note, false))}
   429	              />

   431	        </>
   432	      ) : null}
   433	    </>

   437	export function ClassroomUploadsView() {
   438	  const query = useStudentExternalUploads();
   439	  const uploads = query.data ?? [];
   440	
   441	  return (
   442	    <>
   443	      {/* Index route: no dateline, same reason as Notes and recaps. */}
   444	      <h1 className="classroom-title">Your uploads</h1>

   459	                {upload.processing_status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   460	              </span>

   463	        </section>
   464	      ) : null}

   469	export function ClassroomAssignmentView() {
   470	  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();
   471	  const navigate = useNavigate();
   472	  const assignmentQuery = useStudentAssignment(assignmentId);
   473	  const setQuery = useHomeworkSet(assignmentQuery.assignment?.set_id ?? undefined);
   474	  const assignment = assignmentQuery.assignment;
   475	
   476	  const start = () => {
   477	    if (!assignment) return;
   478	    const params = new URLSearchParams({
   479	      mode: "type-drill",
   480	      qids: assignment.question_qids.join(","),
   481	      assignmentId: assignment.id,
   482	    });
   483	    if (setQuery.set?.id && setQuery.set.is_guided_set) {
   484	      params.set("guidedSetId", setQuery.set.id);
   485	    }
   486	    navigate(`/drill?${params.toString()}`);
   487	  };
   488	
   489	  if (assignmentQuery.loading && !assignment) return <DetailLoading />;
   490	  if (assignmentQuery.error) {
   491	    return (
   492	      <DetailError
   493	        message="Could not load this assignment."
   494	        retry={() => void assignmentQuery.refresh()}
   495	      />
   496	    );
   497	  }
   498	  if (!assignment) {
   499	    return (
   500	      <>
   501	        <DetailHeading
   502	          date={new Date().toISOString()}
   503	          kind="Assignment"
   504	          title="Assignment not found"
   505	        />
   506	      </>
   507	    );
   508	  }
   509	
   510	  const estimatedMinutes =
   511	    setQuery.set?.estimated_minutes ??
   512	    Math.max(1, Math.ceil(assignment.question_qids.length * 2));
   513	
   514	  return (
   515	    <>
   516	      <DetailHeading
   517	        date={assignment.created_at}
   518	        kind="Assignment"
   519	        title={assignment.set_title}
   520	      />
   521	      {assignment.set_description ? (
   522	        <div className="classroom-callout">{assignment.set_description}</div>
   523	      ) : null}
   524	      <div className="classroom-note-meta">
   525	        {assignment.question_qids.length} question
   526	        {assignment.question_qids.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} | about{" "}
   527	        {estimatedMinutes} minutes | {assignment.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   528	      </div>
   529	      {setQuery.error ? (
   530	        <DetailError
   531	          message="Some assignment details could not be loaded."
   532	          retry={() => void setQuery.refresh()}
   533	        />
   534	      ) : null}
   535	      <div className="classroom-detail-actions">
   536	        <button
   537	          type="button"
   538	          className="classroom-button classroom-button-primary"
   539	          disabled={setQuery.loading}
   540	          onClick={start}
   541	        >
   542	          {assignment.status === "completed"
   543	            ? "Review"
   544	            : assignment.status === "in_progress"
   545	              ? "Continue"
   546	              : setQuery.set?.start_button_label || "Start"}
   547	        </button>
   548	      </div>
   549	    </>
   550	  );
   551	}

   553	export function ClassroomTAAssignmentView() {
   554	  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();
   555	  const query = useStudentTAAssignment(assignmentId);
   556	  const assignment = query.assignment;
   557	  const structured = assignment ? hasStructuredWorkspace(assignment) : false;
   558	  const [completing, setCompleting] = React.useState(false);
   559	  const [downloading, setDownloading] = React.useState(false);
   560	  const viewedRef = React.useRef<string | null>(null);
   561	
   562	  React.useEffect(() => {
   563	    if (!assignment || assignment.status !== "assigned") return;
   564	    if (viewedRef.current === assignment.id) return;
   565	    viewedRef.current = assignment.id;
   566	    void markTAAssignmentViewed(assignment.id).then((result) => {
   567	      if (result.ok) void query.refresh();
   568	    });
   569	  }, [assignment, query]);
   570	
   571	  const safeHtml = React.useMemo(
   572	    () => sanitizeClassroomHtml(assignment?.content_html ?? ""),
   573	    [assignment?.content_html],
   574	  );
   575	
   576	  const download = async () => {
   577	    if (!assignment) return;
   578	    setDownloading(true);
   579	    try {
   580	      const { downloadAssignmentPdf } = await import("@/lib/taPdf");
   581	      await downloadAssignmentPdf({
   582	        title: assignment.title,
   583	        contentHtml: safeHtml,
   584	      });
   585	    } catch (error) {
   586	      toast.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "PDF download failed");
   587	    } finally {
   588	      setDownloading(false);
   589	    }
   590	  };
   591	
   592	  const complete = async () => {
   593	    if (!assignment) return;
   594	    setCompleting(true);
   595	    const result = await markTAAssignmentCompleted(assignment.id);
   596	    if (result.ok) {
   597	      toast.success("Marked complete");
   598	      await query.refresh();
   599	    } else {
   600	      toast.error(result.error ?? "Could not mark this complete");
   601	    }
   602	    setCompleting(false);
   603	  };
   604	
   605	  if (query.loading && !assignment) return <DetailLoading />;
   606	  if (query.error) {
   607	    return (
   608	      <DetailError
   609	        message="Could not load this assignment."
   610	        retry={() => void query.refresh()}
   611	      />
   612	    );
   613	  }
   614	  if (!assignment) {
   615	    return (
   616	      <DetailHeading
   617	        date={new Date().toISOString()}
   618	        kind="Assignment"
   619	        title="Assignment not found"
   620	      />
   621	    );
   622	  }
   623	
   624	  return (
   625	    <>
   626	      <DetailHeading
   627	        date={assignment.assigned_at}
   628	        kind="Assignment"
   629	        title={assignment.title}
   630	      />
   631	      <div className="classroom-note-meta">
   632	        {assignment.status.replace(/_/g, " ")}
   633	      </div>
   634	      {safeHtml ? (
   635	        <article
   636	          className="classroom-prose"
   637	          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: safeHtml }}
   638	        />
   639	      ) : null}
   640	      {structured ? (
   641	        <StructuredTAAssignmentWorkspace
   642	          assignment={assignment}
   643	          refresh={query.refresh}
   644	        />
   645	      ) : null}
   646	      <div className="classroom-detail-actions">
   647	        <button
   648	          type="button"
   649	          className="classroom-button"
   650	          disabled={downloading}
   651	          onClick={() => void download()}
   652	        >
   653	          {downloading ? "Preparing PDF" : "Download PDF"}
   654	        </button>
   655	        {!structured && assignment.status !== "completed" ? (
   656	          <button
   657	            type="button"
   658	            className="classroom-button classroom-button-primary"
   659	            disabled={completing}
   660	            onClick={() => void complete()}
   661	          >
   662	            {completing ? "Saving" : "Mark complete"}
   663	          </button>
   664	        ) : null}
   665	      </div>
   666	    </>
   667	  );
   668	}

export async function publishSessionBundle(args: PublishArgs): Promise<PublishResult> {
  const { admin, studentId, draftId, bundle } = args;
  const targets = (args.only ?? unpublishedApprovedParts(bundle)).filter((k) =>
    unpublishedApprovedParts(bundle).includes(k),
  );

  if (targets.length === 0) {
    return { ok: true, published: [], bundle, nothing_to_publish: true };
  }

  // Work on a mutable copy so we can write posted flags back atomically.
  const next: SessionBundle = { ...bundle, parts: { ...bundle.parts } };
  const outcomes: PublishPartOutcome[] = [];

  for (const key of targets) {
    const part = next.parts[key];
    if (!part) continue;
    try {
      const title = (part.title?.trim() || PART_LABELS[key]).slice(0, 200);
      const markdown = partToMarkdown(key, part);

      // An approved part can still render to nothing (body edited blank in
      // review, or items emptied). Posting it would notify the student about a
      // blank recap, so fail this part closed and leave it unposted.
      if (!markdown) {
        outcomes.push({
          part: key,
          ok: false,
          error: `${PART_LABELS[key]} is empty — add content or exclude the part before publishing.`,
        });
        continue;
      }

      if (key === 'session_note') {
        const item = await createClassroomItem({
          studentId,
          createdBy: admin.id,
          type: 'session_recap',
          title,
          contentMarkdown: markdown,
          sourceTable: 'ai_ta_drafts',
          sourceId: draftId,
          status: 'assigned',
          visibleToStudent: true,
          metadata: { bundle_part: key },
        });
        const notify = await notifyStudentOfSessionRecap({
          admin,
          studentId,
          route: itemRoute(item.id),
        });
        outcomes.push({
          part: key,
          ok: true,
          classroom_item_id: item.id,
          notify_summary: notify.inbox_ok && notify.bell_ok
            ? 'Posted and notified'
            : 'Posted, partial notification',
        });
        next.parts[key] = { ...part, posted: true, classroom_item_id: item.id };
      } else {
        // homework + quiz: real assignable items; reuse the assignment notifier.
        const item = await createClassroomItem({
          studentId,
          createdBy: admin.id,
          type: 'homework',
          title,
          contentMarkdown: markdown,
          sourceTable: 'ai_ta_drafts',
          sourceId: draftId,
          status: 'assigned',
          visibleToStudent: true,
          metadata: { bundle_part: key },
        });
        const notify = await notifyStudentOfClassroomItem({
          adminUserId: admin.id,
          studentId,
          title,
          route: itemRoute(item.id),
          description: key === 'quiz' ? 'A short recall check from your session.' : undefined,

## user

Tool result:
 * route resolver and the item view assumed every `homework` row was a mirror of
 * something else: the resolver sent it to `/classroom` and the item view
 * redirected it off its own page, so the button did nothing at all and the
 * direct link bounced back to Home.
 */
export function isSelfContainedItem(item: ClassroomItem): boolean {
  return !item.source_id && !!(item.content_html || item.content_markdown);
}

/**
 * Resolve a classroom_items row to the right student-facing route.
 * Returns the path to navigate to, or null if the item is not actionable.
 */
export function resolveItemRoute(item: ClassroomItem): string | null {
  switch (item.type) {
    case 'drill':
      if (item.source_table === 'guided_drill_assignments' && item.source_id) {
        return `/guided/${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}?returnTo=${encodeURIComponent('/classroom')}`;
      }
      if (item.source_id) return `/drill?assignmentId=${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}`;
      return '/drill';
    case 'homework':
      if (item.source_id) return `/classroom/${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}`;
      if (isSelfContainedItem(item)) return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`;
      return '/classroom';
    case 'activity':
      // source_id is the activity_assignments row id; source_table='activity_assignments'.
      if (item.source_id) return `/activities/${encodeURIComponent(item.source_id)}`;
      return '/activities';
    case 'bootcamp_module':
      if (item.source_id) return item.source_id; // expected to be a /bootcamp/* path
      return '/bootcamps';
    case 'session_recap':
    case 'smartboard_recap':
    case 'announcement':
    case 'note':
      return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`;
    default:
      return `/classroom/item/${encodeURIComponent(item.id)}`;
  }
}

Markdown is newline-significant and\n * `.classroom-prose p` sets no `white-space`, so every recap collapsed into a\n * single run-on paragraph carrying its own syntax: a student read\n * `- [ ] Do PT 141 S2 - [ ] Review your misses` on one line, and\n * `**1. What is a sufficient assumption?**` with the asterisks showing.\n *\n * That body is not hand-typed prose that happens to contain punctuation. It is\n * real markdown by construction: `partToMarkdown` in src/lib/sessionBundle.ts\n * emits `- [ ] `, `**n. prompt**` and `_Answer:_` from the AI TA bundle, and the\n * field it reads is named `body_markdown`.\n *\n * Deliberately narrow, matching RcMarkdown's posture:\n *\n * - Headings demote. A note body sits under the page's own `<h1\n *   class=\"classroom-title\">`, so an authored `#` would open a second document.\n *   h1-h2 render as h2, h3-h6 as h3 — the two levels `.classroom-prose` styles.\n * - Raw HTML is skipped. HTML notes travel the `content_html` path through\n *   `sanitizeClassroomHtml`, which is what allows an authored `<style>` block.\n *   Nothing should reach the DOM from here without passing that.\n * - Task items render as a checkbox glyph rather than the literal `[ ]`.\n *   GitHub task lists need remark-gfm, which this project does not carry; the\n *   bundle emits them regardless, so the marker is translated here instead of\n *   adding a dependency for one syntax.\n * - External links open in a new tab so a note cannot navigate the student out\n *   of the classroom. In-app paths stay in place.\n */\n\nconst TASK_ITEM = /^\\[([ xX])\\]\\s+/;\n\n/** A task item's text arrives as the li's first child on a tight list. */\nfunction splitTaskItem(children: React.ReactNode) {\n  const kids = React.Children.toArray(children);\n  const first = kids[0];\n  if (typeof first !== \"string\") return null;\n  const match = TASK_ITEM.exec(first);\n  if (!match) return null;\n  return {\n    done: match[1].toLowerCase() === \"x\",\n    rest: [first.slice(match[0].length), ...kids.slice(1)],\n  };\n}\n\nconst components: Components = {\n  h1: ({ children }) => <h2>{children}</h2>,\n  h2: ({ children }) => <h2>{children}</h2>,\n  h3: ({ children }) => <h3>{children}</h3>,\n  h4: ({ children }) => <h3>{children}</h3>,\n  h5: ({ children }) => <h3>{children}</h3>,\n  h6: ({ children }) => <h3>{children}</h3>,\n  img: () => null,\n  a: ({ href, children }) => {\n    const external = !!href && /^https?:\\/\\//i.test(href);\n    return (\n      <a\n        href={href}\n        {...(external ? { target: \"_blank\", rel: \"noreferrer noopener\" } : {})}\n      >\n        {children}\n      </a>\n    );\n  },\n  li: ({ children }) => {\n    const task = splitTaskItem(children);\n    if (!task) return <li>{children}</li>;\n    return (\n      <li className=\"classroom-prose-task\" data-done={task.done ? \"\" : undefined}>\n        <span aria-hidden=\"true\" className=\"classroom-prose-task-box\">\n          {task.done ? \"✓\" : \"\"}\n        </span>\n        <span className=\"classroom-prose-task-text\">\n          <span className=\"sr-only\">{task.done ? \"Done: \" : \"To do: \"}</span>\n          {task.rest}\n        </span>\n      </li>\n    );\n  },\n};\n\ninterface Props {\n  children: string | null | undefined;\n  /** The prose container class. Callers outside `.classroom-root` pass their own. */\n  className?: string;\n}\n\nexport function ClassroomMarkdown({ children, className = \"classroom-prose\" }: Props) {\n  if (!children || !children.trim()) return null;\n  return (\n    <article className={className}>\n      <ReactMarkdown components={components} skipHtml>\n        {children}\n      </ReactMarkdown>\n    </article>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default ClassroomMarkdown;\nEOF\necho written", "description": "Create ClassroomMarkdown renderer"}

): Promise<void> {
  const patch: { status: ClassroomItem['status']; completed_at?: string } = { status };
  if (status === 'completed') patch.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();
  const { error } = await supabase
    .from('classroom_items')
    .update(patch)
    .eq('source_table', sourceTable)
    .eq('source_id', sourceId);
  if (error) throw error;
}

export function nextActionFor(items: ClassroomItem[]): ClassroomItem | null {
  if (!items?.length) return null;
  const candidates = items.filter(
    (i) => i.status === 'assigned' || i.status === 'in_progress',
  );
  if (candidates.length === 0) return null;
  // Highest priority, then earliest due date, then earliest unlock.
  return [...candidates].sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.priority !== a.priority) return b.priority - a.priority;
    const adue = a.due_at ? new Date(a.due_at).getTime() : Infinity;
    const bdue = b.due_at ? new Date(b.due_at).getTime() : Infinity;
    if (adue !== bdue) return adue - bdue;
    const aun = a.unlock_at ? new Date(a.unlock_at).getTime() : 0;
    const bun = b.unlock_at ? new Date(b.unlock_at).getTime() : 0;
    return aun - bun;
  })[0];
}

/**
 * An assignment that carries its own page rather than pointing at one.
 *
 * A timed section is written straight into `classroom_items` with its cover
 * page in `content_html` and no backing assignment row, because there is no
 * assignment table for "sit PT 155 section 1 on a 53 minute clock". Both the
 * route resolver and the item view assumed every `homework` row was a mirror of
 * something else: the resolver sent it to `/classroom` and the item view
 * redirected it off its own page, so the button did nothing at all and the
 * direct link bounced back to Home.
 */
export function isSelfContainedItem(item: ClassroomItem): boolean {
  return !item.source_id && !!(item.content_html || item.content_markdown);
}

/**
 * Resolve a classroom_items row to the right student-facing route.
 * Returns the path to navigate to, or null if the item is not actionable.
 */

    case 'drill':

function stripMarkdown(source: string) {

    .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, " ")
    .replace(/`([^`]*)`/g, "$1")

}

59	/**
60	 * Markdown reduced to the words it renders, for a plain-text excerpt.
61	 *
62	 * The excerpt is the only place a recap body is shown without going through
63	 * ClassroomMarkdown, so the syntax has to come off here instead. Without this
64	 * the featured note on the landing surface opened with `## What we worked on`
65	 * or `- [ ] Redo PT 141 S2` — the exact leak the reading view was just fixed
66	 * for. Structural markers are dropped; the words they wrapped are kept.
67	 */
68	function stripMarkdown(source: string) {
69	  return source
70	    .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, " ")
71	    .replace(/`([^`]*)`/g, "$1")

73	    .replace(/\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]*\)/g, "$1")
74	    .replace(/^\s{0,3}>\s?/gm, "")

80	}

85	 * Notes are authored HTML and may carry their own <style>, so the tags are
86	 * stripped through a detached element rather than a regex, and any <style> or
87	 * <script> subtree is dropped first so CSS text never leaks into the excerpt.

function stripMarkdown(source: string) {
  return source
    .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, " ")
    .replace(/`([^`]*)`/g, "$1")
    .replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g, " ")
    .replace(/\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]*\)/g, "$1")
    .replace(/^\s{0,3}>\s?/gm, "")
    .replace(/^\s{0,3}#{1,6}\s+/gm, "")
    .replace(/^[ \t]*(?:[-*+]|\d+\.)[ \t]+(?:\[[ xX]\][ \t]+)?/gm, "")
    .replace(/^[ \t]*(?:-{3,}|\*{3,}|_{3,})[ \t]*$/gm, " ")
    .replace(/(\*\*|__)(.+?)\1/g, "$2")
    .replace(/(?<![\w*])([*_])(?!\s)(.+?)(?<!\s)\1(?![\w*])/g, "$2");
}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\src\\components\\classroom\\ClassroomMarkdown.test.tsx", "content": "import { render, screen } from \"@testing-library/react\";\nimport { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport ClassroomMarkdown from \"./ClassroomMarkdown\";\n\n/**\n * The bug these lock down: `content_markdown` was rendered as\n * `<p>{item.content_markdown}</p>`, so every recap reached the student as one\n * run-on line carrying its own syntax.\n */\ndescribe(\"a recap body written in markdown\", () => {\n  it(\"renders paragraphs as separate blocks rather than one run-on line\", () => {\n    const { container } = render(\n      <ClassroomMarkdown>{\"First thing.\\n\\nSecond thing.\"}</ClassroomMarkdown>,\n    );\n    const paragraphs = container.querySelectorAll(\"p\");\n    expect(paragraphs).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(paragraphs[0].textContent).toBe(\"First thing.\");\n    expect(paragraphs[1].textContent).toBe(\"Second thing.\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders a bullet list as a list, not as text starting with a dash\", () => {\n    const { container } = render(\n      <ClassroomMarkdown>{\"- Sufficient assumption\\n- Negation\"}</ClassroomMarkdown>,\n    );\n    expect(container.querySelectorAll(\"li\")).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(container.textContent).not.toContain(\"- Sufficient\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders emphasis rather than printing the asterisks\", () => {\n    const { container } = render(\n      <ClassroomMarkdown>{\"**1. What is a sufficient assumption?**\"}</ClassroomMarkdown>,\n    );\n    expect(container.querySelector(\"strong\")?.textContent).toBe(\n      \"1. What is a sufficient assumption?\",\n    );\n    expect(container.textContent).not.toContain(\"**\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"demotes an authored h1 so it cannot open a second document\", () => {\n    // The page already renders the title as <h1 class=\"classroom-title\">.\n    const { container } = render(\n      <ClassroomMarkdown>{\"# What we covered\"}</ClassroomMarkdown>,\n    );\n    expect(container.querySelector(\"h1\")).toBeNull();\n    expect(container.querySelector(\"h2\")?.textContent).toBe(\"What we covered\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders a task item as a box rather than the literal [ ]\", () => {\n    // partToMarkdown emits exactly this shape for a bundle's homework part.\n    const { container } = render(\n      <ClassroomMarkdown>{\"- [ ] Redo PT 141 S2\\n- [x] Blind review\"}</ClassroomMarkdown>,\n    );\n    const tasks = container.querySelectorAll(\"li.classroom-prose-task\");\n    expect(tasks).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(container.textContent).not.toContain(\"[ ]\");\n    expect(container.textContent).not.toContain(\"[x]\");\n    expect(tasks[0].hasAttribute(\"data-done\")).toBe(false);\n    expect(tasks[1].hasAttribute(\"data-done\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(screen.getByText(\"Redo PT 141 S2\")).toBeTruthy();\n  });\n\n  it(\"does not let raw HTML through; that path belongs to the sanitizer\", () => {\n    const { container } = render(\n      <ClassroomMarkdown>{\"<style>body{display:none}</style>Hello\"}</ClassroomMarkdown>,\n    );\n    expect(container.querySelector(\"style\")).toBeNull();\n    expect(container.textContent).toContain(\"Hello\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"sends an external link to a new tab and leaves an in-app path alone\", () => {\n    const { container } = render(\n      <ClassroomMarkdown>\n        {\"[7Sage](https://7sage.com) and [your drills](/classroom/drill)\"}\n      </ClassroomMarkdown>,\n    );\n    const [external, internal] = [...container.querySelectorAll(\"a\")];\n    expect(external.getAttribute(\"target\")).toBe(\"_blank\");\n    expect(external.getAttribute(\"rel\")).toContain(\"noopener\");\n    expect(internal.getAttribute(\"target\")).toBeNull();\n  });\n\n  it(\"renders nothing for an empty or whitespace-only body\", () => {\n    const { container } = render(<ClassroomMarkdown>{\"   \\n  \"}</ClassroomMarkdown>);\n    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n"}

{"action": "javascript_exec", "tabId": "tab-1", "text": "const css = await fetch('/src/components/classroom/classroom.css?direct').then(r => r.text());\nconst style = document.createElement('style');\nstyle.id = 'probe-css';\nstyle.textContent = css;\ndocument.head.appendChild(style);\n\nconst host = document.createElement('div');\nhost.className = 'classroom-root';\nhost.innerHTML = `\n  <div style=\"background:var(--classroom-page)\">\n    <article class=\"classroom-prose\" id=\"probe-prose\">\n      <p>Read the <a href=\"https://7sage.com\" id=\"probe-link\">analytics page</a> before Thursday.</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li class=\"classroom-prose-task\" id=\"probe-task\"><span class=\"classroom-prose-task-box\">&nbsp;</span><span class=\"classroom-prose-task-text\">Redo PT 141 S2</span></li>\n      </ul>\n      <h2 id=\"probe-h2\">What we covered</h2>\n      <h3 id=\"probe-h3\">Next</h3>\n    </article>\n  </div>`;\ndocument.body.appendChild(host);\n\nconst g = id => getComputedStyle(document.getElementById(id));\nconst link = g('probe-link');\nconst task = g('probe-task');\nconst box = g('classroom-root' && 'probe-task').display;\n({\n  cssBytes: css.length,\n  linkColor: link.color,\n  linkDecoration: link.textDecorationLine,\n  taskDisplay: task.display,\n  taskListStyle: task.listStyleType,\n  boxBorder: getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('#probe-task .classroom-prose-task-box')).borderTopColor,\n  h2Size: g('probe-h2').fontSize,\n  h3Size: g('probe-h3').fontSize,\n  page: getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.classroom-root')).getPropertyValue('--classroom-page').trim(),\n  linkTextToken: getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.classroom-root')).getPropertyValue('--classroom-link-text').trim(),\n})"}

{"action": "javascript_exec", "tabId": "tab-1", "text": "function lum(rgb){const [r,g,b]=rgb.match(/\\d+/g).map(Number).map(v=>{v/=255;return v<=0.03928?v/12.92:Math.pow((v+0.055)/1.055,2.4);});return 0.2126*r+0.7152*g+0.0722*b;}\nfunction ratio(a,b){const L1=lum(a),L2=lum(b);return +(((Math.max(L1,L2)+0.05)/(Math.min(L1,L2)+0.05)).toFixed(2));}\nconst root=document.querySelector('.classroom-root');\nconst link=getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('probe-link')).color;\nconst body=getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('probe-prose')).color;\nconst box=getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('#probe-task .classroom-prose-task-box')).borderTopColor;\nconst light={ page:'rgb(255,255,255)', link, body, box };\n\n// dark: the layout sets html.dark\ndocument.documentElement.classList.add('dark');\nconst darkLink=getComputedStyle(document.getElementById('probe-link')).color;\nconst darkPage=getComputedStyle(root).getPropertyValue('--classroom-page').trim();\nconst darkBox=getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('#probe-task .classroom-prose-task-box')).borderTopColor;\ndocument.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');\n\n({\n  light_linkOnPage: ratio(link,'rgb(255,255,255)'),\n  light_bodyOnPage: ratio(body,'rgb(255,255,255)'),\n  light_taskBoxOnPage: ratio(box,'rgb(255,255,255)'),\n  old_ff8906_onPage: ratio('rgb(255,137,6)','rgb(255,255,255)'),\n  dark_page: darkPage,\n  dark_link: darkLink,\n  dark_linkOnPage: ratio(darkLink,'rgb(15,14,23)'),\n  dark_taskBoxOnPage: ratio(darkBox,'rgb(15,14,23)'),\n})"}

## user

Tool result:
`.classroom-mobile-bar-trigger`, the sticky bar’s section name promoted to a button. The
bar is sticky where the trigger was not, it names the section where the trigger said only
"Open the list", and it puts the room control and the section control in one row instead
of stacking two identically styled boxes that a student could not tell apart. The
index/detail split is `activeSection && activeId` in React rather than
`:has(.classroom-stage-index)` in CSS: an index route keeps a plain label, because a
control that opens a copy of what is on screen is still the no-op this shell removed once
before.

**A control whose border is its only edge uses `--classroom-line-strong`.** The warm
hairline `--classroom-line` measures 1.25:1 against the page and `--classroom-trigger`
measures 1.03:1, so a button built from that pair has nothing at 3:1 telling a student it
is a button. PRODUCT.md makes 3:1 on the visual boundary of an interactive control a
binding floor. `--classroom-line-strong` exists for this and measures 3.14:1 light and
3.21:1 dark; before 2026-09-09 it appeared once in 5,101 lines. Every interactive control
in this stylesheet now uses it: the mobile menu button, both `.classroom-button` variants,
sign-out, the overlay close, the drawer search, the account panel’s inputs, select and
ghost button, the announcement button, the toggle track and its knob, the journal and
review choices,
the textarea, the review control, and the five orientation controls. A rule is a control
if it carries `cursor: pointer`, `resize`, or an input/select/textarea selector; a scan on
that test returns zero controls left on the hairline.

**`--classroom-line-strong` is measured against the page and the panel, so a control on a
tinted ground takes `--classroom-line-on-tint` instead.** The two figures above are the
ratios against `--classroom-page` and `--classroom-surface`. They do not carry over to a
control sitting on something else. The toggle knob is the one case: it sits on the track,
which is `--classroom-hover` composited to `rgb(251,247,243)` light and `rgb(33,31,35)`
dark, and `--classroom-line-strong` read 2.68:1 there while reading 3.14:1 six pixels
away on the panel. Note the direction reverses between themes - the tinted ground is
darker than the panel on light and lighter than it on dark, so the token is darker than
`--classroom-line-strong` on light and lighter on dark. Measured 2026-09-09 on the
production build: knob border 3.75:1 against the track in both themes, 3.99:1 light and
4.55:1 dark against the knob fill. Before adding a third border token, check whether the
control's ground is genuinely a new one; two is the whole set.

`--classroom-line` stays what it always was: hairline separation between things, not the
edge of a control. It still carries the cards, the panels, the callouts, the prose media,
`.classroom-choice` (an `<li>` in an `<ol>`, not a button), `.classroom-review-correct-answer`
(a read-only display box, split out of the rule it used to share with the live control),
`.classroom-orientation-list-row` and `.classroom-orientation-day` (both `<div>`s).

**A border-colour change is not a focus indicator.** Three fields shipped
`outline: none` with only a border tint underneath: `.classroom-textarea`,
`.classroom-orientation-input` and `.classroom-review-control`. PRODUCT.md asks for a
visible focus indicator on every interactive element, and a tint that is now the same
colour as the rest border is no indicator at all. All three draw
`outline: 2px solid var(--classroom-ink)` on `:focus-visible`, the pattern
`.classroom-panel-search` already documented. No `outline: none` remains in this file.

**A focus ring uses `--classroom-link-text`, not `--classroom-link`.** The decorative
orange `#ff8906` measures 2.38:1 on the light ground, under the 3:1 a focus indicator
owes. `--classroom-link-text` is the themed pair that passes: 5.38:1 on light, the same
`#ff8906` on the near-black dark ground. `.classroom-icon-button` and
`.classroom-overlay-close` still ring in `--classroom-link` and are the open case.

**Overlays are dialogs.** The section drawer and the mobile menu go through
`useDialogOverlay`: `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, an accessible name, focus moved in on
open, a focus trap that wraps both ways, Escape, and focus restored to the trigger. Focus
is moved on a timer rather than `requestAnimationFrame`, because rAF does not fire while
the document is not being rendered.

**Index routes carry no dateline.** It printed `new Date()`, so it stamped the page with
the day of the visit while occupying the slot that, on a note, holds the day Joshua wrote
it. Every number on a surface traces to stored data or it does not appear.

**The 44px touch floor is scoped to touch, and this is not a deviation.** `.classroom-nav`
computes to 34px on a fine pointer and grows to 44px under `@media (pointer: coarse)` and
inside the mobile drawer. PRODUCT.md's binding floor is WCAG 2.1 AA; 44x44 is 2.5.5,
which is Level AAA, while the AA target-size rule is 2.2's 2.5.8 at 24x24. A 34px row
clears the binding floor. PRODUCT.md line 104 lists the AAA figure inside its AA section,
which is a doc imprecision to fix there, not a defect here. Do not raise this as a
violation, and do not attribute a waiver to Joshua - there is nothing to waive.

**The switch's on-track is its own token, because `--classroom-active` was too close
to the off-track on dark.** Off-track versus on-track measured 9.68:1 on light but
1.51:1 on dark: `--classroom-hover` over the dark panel lands at `rgb(33,31,35)` and
`--classroom-active` is `#3d3c48`, so the 16px knob translation was carrying the state
by itself. Not a 1.4.11 failure, since position is non-colour information, but state
should not rest on one channel in one theme only. `--classroom-toggle-on-track` is
`#43403c` on light, which is `--classroom-active` unchanged, so light renders exactly as
before; dark is `#6f6d80`, a lighter sibling in the same hue. Scoped to this control so
the other 17 `--classroom-active` rules keep their colour. Measured 2026-09-09 on the
production build: off-vs-on 3.25:1 dark and 9.68:1 light, with the on-state knob at
3.93:1 against its track.

**`smartboard_recap` is a dead label.** Boards are not attached to student accounts
(confirmed 2026-09-09), so no student sees it. The mapping stays only so an unmapped
value cannot fall through to the raw enum. Not worth wording.

## Anti-references

Not a dashboard. Not an LMS. No progress rings, no streak badges, no card grid of
modules, no metric tiles. The classroom is a room with a desk in it, and the thing on the
desk is what the coach wrote.

## user

Tool result:
function RecapRow({ recap }: { recap: ClassroomItem }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const body = recap.content_markdown ?? '';

  return (
    <li className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/40">
      <div className="px-4 py-3">
        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-100">{recap.title}</div>
        <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 mt-0.5">
          {format(new Date(recap.created_at), 'MMM d, yyyy')}
        </div>
        {body && (
          <div
            className={
              open
                ? 'mt-2 prose prose-sm prose-invert max-w-none prose-p:my-2 prose-ul:my-2'
                : 'mt-2 prose prose-sm prose-invert max-w-none prose-p:my-2 prose-ul:my-2 line-clamp-4'
            }
          >
            <ClassroomMarkdown className="">{body}</ClassroomMarkdown>
          </div>
        )}
        {body && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
            className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-zinc-100"
            aria-expanded={open}
          >
            {open ? 'Show less' : 'Read the whole thing'}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </li>
  );
}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\src\\components\\teacher\\PostedRecapsList.tsx", "content": "import { useState } from 'react';\nimport { format } from 'date-fns';\nimport { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';\nimport ClassroomMarkdown, {\n  TEACHER_MARKDOWN_CLASS,\n} from '@/components/classroom/ClassroomMarkdown';\nimport {\n  useStudentClassroomItems,\n  type ClassroomItem,\n} from '@/hooks/useClassroomItems';\n\n/**\n * What has already been posted to a student's Notes and recaps, read the way\n * the student reads it.\n *\n * The dossier could post a recap and could not show one. `TeacherRecaps` was\n * the only screen that listed them and nothing routes to it, so once a recap\n * went out the only way to check it was to be the student. That matters more\n * than it sounds: the body is markdown, the classroom renders it as markdown,\n * and until now neither side of the app showed it rendered.\n */\n\n/** The types that land under Notes and recaps on the student's side. */\nconst POSTED_TYPES = new Set<ClassroomItem['type']>([\n  'session_recap',\n  'smartboard_recap',\n  'note',\n  'announcement',\n]);\n\nconst TYPE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {\n  session_recap: 'Session recap',\n  smartboard_recap: 'Board',\n  note: 'Note',\n  announcement: 'Announcement',\n};\n\nexport function PostedRecapRow({ recap }: { recap: ClassroomItem }) {\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\n  const body = recap.content_markdown ?? '';\n  const kind = TYPE_LABELS[recap.type] ?? recap.type.replace(/_/g, ' ');\n\n  return (\n    <li className=\"rounded-lg border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/40 px-4 py-3\">\n      <div className=\"text-sm font-medium text-zinc-100\">{recap.title}</div>\n      <div className=\"mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-500\">\n        {kind} · {format(new Date(recap.created_at), 'MMM d, yyyy')}\n        {recap.visible_to_student ? '' : ' · hidden from the student'}\n      </div>\n      {body && (\n        <div className={open ? 'mt-2' : 'mt-2 line-clamp-4'}>\n          <ClassroomMarkdown className={TEACHER_MARKDOWN_CLASS}>\n            {body}\n          </ClassroomMarkdown>\n        </div>\n      )}\n      {body && (\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}\n          className=\"mt-2 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 underline underline-offset-2 hover:text-zinc-100\"\n          aria-expanded={open}\n        >\n          {open ? 'Show less' : 'Read the whole thing'}\n        </button>\n      )}\n    </li>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function PostedRecapsList({ studentId }: { studentId: string | null }) {\n  const { data: items = [], isLoading, error } = useStudentClassroomItems(studentId);\n  const posted = items.filter((i) => POSTED_TYPES.has(i.type));\n\n  if (!studentId) return null;\n  if (isLoading) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-zinc-500\">\n        <Loader2 className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin\" /> Loading…\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"text-[12px] text-zinc-500\">\n        Could not load what has been posted.\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  if (posted.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"text-[12px] text-zinc-500\">\n        Nothing posted yet. \"Post a session recap\" above puts one in their\n        Classroom.\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n      {posted.map((recap) => (\n        <PostedRecapRow key={recap.id} recap={recap} />\n      ))}\n    </ul>\n  );\n}\n"}

Markdown is\nnewline-significant and .classroom-prose p sets no white-space, so a recap\nreached the student as one run-on paragraph carrying its own syntax:\n\"- [ ] Redo PT 141 S2 - [ ] Blind review\" on a single line, and\n\"**1. What is a sufficient assumption?**\" with the asterisks showing.\n\nThe bodies are markdown by construction, not prose that happens to contain\npunctuation. partToMarkdown emits \"- [ ] \", \"**n. prompt**\" and \"_Answer:_\"\nfrom a field named body_markdown, and every AI TA session bundle goes through\nit. react-markdown was already a dependency.\n\nClassroomMarkdown is the one renderer. Headings demote to the h2/h3 the\nstylesheet styles, so an authored \"#\" cannot open a second document under the\npage's own h1. Raw HTML is skipped, because that path belongs to\nsanitizeClassroomHtml and its <style> allowance. Task markers render as a box\nrather than the literal [ ], since the bundle emits them and remark-gfm is not\nin this project. External links open in a new tab; in-app paths stay put.\n\nThe landing excerpt strips the same syntax rather than rendering it: it is the\none place a body is shown without going through the renderer, and it was\nopening notes with \"## What we covered\".\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nMSG", "description": "Commit markdown renderer"}

That is the text/fill split that held every date below the\nfloor for months, still live in the one place a student reads what their coach\nwrote. --classroom-link-text measures 5.38:1 light and 8.06:1 dark. The rule\nalso set text-decoration: none and moved the colour to --classroom-ink on\nhover, so a link was identifiable by colour alone and stopped being\nidentifiable at all under the cursor. Underlined in both states now.\n\nThe list markers were gone. Tailwind's preflight sets\n\"ol, ul, menu { list-style: none }\" app-wide and this stylesheet restored only\nthe indent, so every list in every note rendered as unmarked indented lines and\na numbered list lost its numbers. That is a change of meaning, not of style:\n\"1, 2, 3\" became three lines that no longer said they were in order.\nlist-style-type computed \"none\" on a note's <ul>; disc, decimal and circle are\ndeclared explicitly now.\n\nTask items get the box the renderer's markup expects, on\n--classroom-line-strong at 3.14:1 light and 3.21:1 dark.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nMSG", "description": "Commit CSS fixes"}

The body is stored as markdown and the classroom\nrenders it as markdown, so the only place to find out what a heading or a\nbullet list looked like was the student's own screen, after posting. It is also\na real dialog now: role, aria-modal, an accessible name, focus into the title\nfield, and Escape that asks before discarding a half-written recap.\n\nThe dossier could post a recap and could not show one. TeacherRecaps was the\nonly screen that listed them and nothing routes to it, so once a recap went out\nthe only way to check it was to be the student. PostedRecapsList puts what has\nbeen posted under the composer, rendered through the same ClassroomMarkdown the\nclassroom uses, so a heading that looks wrong here looks wrong there.\n\nNot styled with \"prose prose-sm prose-invert\". @tailwindcss/typography is in\npackage.json but is not registered in tailwind.config.ts, so every prose* class\nin this codebase emits nothing: zero .prose rules exist in the app's\nstylesheets, and a rendered h2 computes to the inherited body colour at 16px.\nArbitrary variants are core Tailwind and actually emit. The four other surfaces\nusing prose classes are untouched and still unstyled.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nMSG", "description": "Commit teacher-side changes"}

It\nasks now, naming what it is about to remove.\n\nThe pending flag was global: remove.isPending greyed out every trash icon in\nboth lists while one delete was in flight. It tracks the row instead.\n\nAnd a note was two lines of preview and nothing else, so the rest of what was\nwritten had no reader anywhere in the app. Rows that carry more than their\npreview expand.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nMSG", "description": "Commit StudentNotes changes"}

{"command": "gh pr create --base main --head notes-and-recaps --title \"Notes and recaps: render what the coach wrote\" --body-file - <<'BODY' 2>&1 | tail -5\nSix commits on the Notes and recaps surface, student side and teacher side. All measured or tested, not inferred.\n\n## What was wrong\n\n**A recap was printed, not rendered.** `content_markdown` went through `<p>{item.content_markdown}</p>`. Markdown is newline-significant and `.classroom-prose p` sets no `white-space`, so a recap arrived as one run-on paragraph carrying its own syntax. This is not a hypothetical: `partToMarkdown` emits `- [ ] `, `**n. prompt**` and `_Answer:_` from a field named `body_markdown`, and every AI TA session bundle publishes through it.\n\n**Lists had no markers.** Tailwind's preflight sets `ol, ul, menu { list-style: none }` app-wide and `.classroom-prose` restored only the indent. A numbered list lost its numbers, which changes meaning.\n\n**A link in a note measured 2.38:1** on `--classroom-link`, with `text-decoration: none` and a hover that moved it to the plain ink colour.\n\n**The homework attached to a recap was unreachable.** `publishSessionBundle` stamps every bundle part with `source_table: 'ai_ta_drafts'`; `resolveItemRoute` read the bare `source_id` and sent the student to `/classroom/<draft uuid>`, where the assignment view rendered \"Assignment not found\".\n\n**Recaps were posted blind.** No preview in the composer, and no list of what had already been posted on the live teacher surface — `TeacherRecaps` has no route.\n\n**A note or transcript deleted on one click**, no confirm, no undo.\n\n## Measured\n\n| | before | after |\n|---|---|---|\n| note link, light | 2.38:1 | **5.38:1** |\n| note link, dark | — | **8.06:1** |\n| task box on page | — | 3.14:1 light / 3.21:1 dark |\n| `<ul>` list-style-type | `none` | `disc` |\n\nTaken in the browser against the served stylesheet with transitions disabled, both themes.\n\n## Verified\n\n- `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` — exit 0\n- `npx eslint` on all changed files — exit 0\n- `npx vitest run src/components/classroom src/lib/classroomItems*` — 14 files, 133 tests, exit 0 (8 new for the renderer, 3 new for the routing fix)\n- `ClassroomMarkdown` and `RecapComposer` mounted from source in a real browser against the real stylesheet; markup, computed styles and both themes checked\n\nNot verified: nothing behind a login. This session could not authenticate, so `/classroom/recaps` and the dossier were not walked end to end as a signed-in user.\n\n## Not done, deliberately\n\n- `@tailwindcss/typography` is a dependency but is **not registered in `tailwind.config.ts`**, so every `prose*` class in this repo emits nothing — zero `.prose` rules exist in the app's stylesheets. Four other surfaces rely on those classes (`OperatorChat`, `DraftReplyCard`, `DeskChatPanel`, `TutorCorpus`) and are still unstyled. This PR sidesteps it rather than registering the plugin, which would change all four at once.\n- Three files on this surface have no importers: `src/pages/ClassroomRecaps.tsx`, `src/components/classroom/RecapsSection.tsx`, `src/pages/ClassroomItem.tsx`. Left on disk; the comment that cited one of them as a source of truth is corrected.\n- Three remaining `--classroom-link` uses that carry words, all outside the reading column: `.classroom-message-link`, `.classroom-annotate-button-active`, `.classroom-orientation-eyebrow`. Named in the surface brief.\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nBODY", "description": "Open pull request"}

Any\nhand-written prose stylesheet that sets only `padding-left` gets indented lines with no\nbullets, and a numbered list loses its numbers entirely. `.classroom-prose` shipped that\nway until 2026-09-09.\n\n**Why:** losing numbers is a change of meaning, not of style — \"1, 2, 3\" becomes three\nlines that no longer say they are in order. It is invisible in review because the CSS\nlooks complete.\n\n**How to apply:** when writing or auditing prose CSS anywhere in this repo, check\n`getComputedStyle(ul).listStyleType` in the browser rather than assuming the browser\ndefault. Declare `disc` / `decimal` explicitly.\n\nRelated: [[tailwind-prose-classes-are-inert]]\n"}

Done. PR is up: [vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db#108](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/pull/108) — six commits on `notes-and-recaps`.

## The headline defect

A recap was **printed, not rendered**. [ClassroomRouteViews.tsx](projects/lsatu/src/components/classroom/ClassroomRouteViews.tsx) put `content_markdown` through `<p>{item.content_markdown}</p>`. Markdown is newline-significant and `.classroom-prose p` sets no `white-space`, so a recap reached the student as one run-on paragraph carrying its own syntax.

This isn't theoretical. `partToMarkdown` in [sessionBundle.ts:116](projects/lsatu/src/lib/sessionBundle.ts:116) emits `- [ ] `, `**n. prompt**` and `_Answer:_` out of a field literally named `body_markdown`, and every AI TA session bundle publishes through it. `react-markdown` was already a dependency.

## Everything else it turned up

| Found | State |
|---|---|
| Lists had **no markers** — Tailwind preflight strips `list-style` app-wide and `.classroom-prose` restored only the indent. A numbered list lost its numbers. | fixed |
| A link in a note measured **2.38:1**, `text-decoration: none`, hover moving it to plain ink | fixed — 5.38:1 light, 8.06:1 dark, underlined both states |
| **The homework posted with a recap was unreachable.** `publishSessionBundle` stamps parts with `source_table: 'ai_ta_drafts'`; `resolveItemRoute` read the bare `source_id` and sent students to `/classroom/<draft uuid>` → "Assignment not found" | fixed + 3 tests |
| You post recaps **blind** — no preview, and the dossier could post one but never show one back (`TeacherRecaps` has no route) | fixed — preview in the composer, "Posted to their Classroom" in the dossier, same renderer both sides |
| A note or transcript **deleted on one click**, no confirm, no undo; and a note could only ever be read two lines deep | fixed |

## Verified

`tsc` exit 0 · `eslint` exit 0 on all changed files · **133 tests pass** (11 new). Both components mounted from source in a real browser against the real stylesheet, both themes, computed styles measured with transitions disabled.

**Not verified:** anything behind a login. I can't authenticate, so `/classroom/recaps` and the dossier weren't walked as a signed-in user.

## Three things for you

1. **`@tailwindcss/typography` is installed but never registered in `tailwind.config.ts`.** Zero `.prose` rules exist in the app's stylesheets — every `prose`/`prose-invert` class in the repo emits nothing. `OperatorChat`, `DraftReplyCard`, `DeskChatPanel` and `TutorCorpus` have been rendering unstyled markdown. I sidestepped it rather than registering the plugin and changing four surfaces at once. Want me to?

2. **Four files on this surface have no importers:** `pages/ClassroomRecaps.tsx`, `RecapsSection.tsx`, `pages/ClassroomItem.tsx`, `pages/teacher/TeacherRecaps.tsx`. One was cited in a live comment as the shared source of the empty-state copy — that comment is corrected. Files left on disk; say the word and I'll remove them.

3. I worked in a separate worktree (`../lsatu-wt-notes`) because another session switched this checkout from `rrc-step-8` to `rrc-step-10` mid-task. I restored your main checkout to their state, but **four new untracked files of mine are still sitting in it** (`ClassroomMarkdown.tsx`, its test, `PostedRecapsList.tsx`, `teacherMarkdown.ts`). They're all safely on the branch. Want me to delete those copies so the other session can't sweep them into an RRC commit?

I didn't raise the `questionStatsBuilder.test.ts` load failure first as the handoff suggested — your instruction redirected to this. It's still open and still silently skipping a whole suite on `main`.
